                   ==>

                   ==>

                              -12-

                   ==>

                   ==>

                   ==>

                 ==>

67                   TILE_LOAD_SIZE_4B: load_cmd.byte_count = 4;
                     ==>
68                   TILE_LOAD_SIZE_6B: load_cmd.byte_count = 6;
                     ==>
69                   TILE_LOAD_SIZE_8B: load_cmd.byte_count = 8;
                     ==>
70                   TILE_LOAD_SIZE_12B: load_cmd.byte_count = 12;
                     ==>
71                   TILE_LOAD_SIZE_16B: load_cmd.byte_count = 16;
                     ==>
72                   default: ;
                     ==>
73                 endcase
74                 case (tile_loadb_form_e'(b[0][3:1]))
                   -4-  
75                   TILE_LOADB_BITS, TILE_LOADB_BITS_NEAR: begin
76                     load_cmd.form = b[0][1] ? LOAD_BITS_NEAR : LOAD_BITS;
                                               -5-  
                                               ==>  
                                               ==>  
77                     load_cmd.byte_count = 1;
78                     load_cmd.align_new = 0;
79                   end
80                   TILE_LOADB_DATA, TILE_LOADB_DATA_NEAR: begin
81                     load_cmd.form = b[0][1] ? LOAD_DATA_NEAR : LOAD_DATA;
                                               -6-  
                                               ==>  
                                               ==>  
82                     load_cmd.capture_addr = b[1][0];
83                   end
84                   TILE_LOADB_DATA_EXT: begin
85                     load_cmd.byte_count = {1'b0,b[1][7:2]} + 7'd1;
                       ==>
86                     load_cmd.last_bits_m1 = TILE_BYTE_IDX'(TILE_BYTE_WIDTH-1);
87                     load_cmd.capture_addr = b[1][0];
88                   end
89                   TILE_LOADB_INDEX: begin
90                     load_cmd.kind = LOAD_INDEX_VALUE;
                       ==>
91                     load_cmd.byte_count = {5'b0,b[1][7:6]} + 7'd1;
92                   end
93                   default: ;
                     ==>
94                 endcase
95               end
96               TILE_OP_SETM: begin
97                 mm = b[0][3:1];
                   ==>
98                 flag0 = b[0][0];
99                 compute_cmd.execute.params.arg.bit_offset = {2'b0,b[1][7:5]};
100                mask_nibble = b[1][4:1];
101              end
102              TILE_OP_LOGU, TILE_OP_LOGL: begin
103                rb = {2'b0,b[0][3:1]};
104                rc = {2'b0,b[1][7:5]};
105                mm = b[1][4:2];
106                if (tile_opcode_e'(b[0][7:4]) == TILE_OP_LOGU) begin
                   -7-  
107                  compute_cmd.execute.logic_payload.uniform.table_bits = b[2];
                     ==>
108                  compute_cmd.execute.logic_payload.uniform.lane_en = b[3];
109                  flag0 = b[0][0];
110                end else begin
111                  compute_cmd.execute.logic_payload.functions = {b[2],b[3],b[4]};
                     ==>
112                  dst_reg = b[1][0];
113                  rd = b[5][7:3];
114                end
115              end
116              TILE_OP_ST: begin
117                dst_reg = 1;
                   ==>
118                rd = b[1][7:3];
119              end
120              TILE_OP_LDX: begin
121                flag0 = b[0][3];
                   ==>
122                // The five-bit field encodes width-1, not the high endpoint.
123                compute_cmd.execute.params.field_width = {1'b0,b[1][1:0],b[2][7:5]} + $bits(compute_cmd.execute.params.field_width)'(unsigned'(1));
124                compute_cmd.execute.params.arg.bit_offset = b[2][4:0];
125              end
126              TILE_OP_STI: begin
127                dst_reg = 1;
                   ==>
128                rd = b[1][7:3];
129                compute_cmd.execute.params.arg.bit_offset = b[2][7:3];
130              end
131              TILE_OP_ROT: begin
132                ra = {2'b0,b[0][3:1]};
                   ==>
133                compute_cmd.execute.params.arg.bit_offset = b[1][7:3];
134              end
135              TILE_OP_ARITH: begin
136                flag0 = b[0][3];
                   ==>
137                flag1 = b[1][2];
138                compute_cmd.execute.params.group_code = b[0][2:1];
139                mm = b[2][2:0];
140                dst_reg = b[1][1];
141              end
142              TILE_OP_CMP, TILE_OP_SHIFT: begin
143                compute_cmd.execute.params.group_code = b[0][1:0];
                   ==>
144                compute_cmd.execute.params.fn = compute_function_u'(b[0][3:2]);
145                flag0 = b[0][3];
146                flag1 = b[0][2];
147                mm = b[2][2:0];
148                dst_reg = b[1][2];
149              end
150              TILE_OP_RED: begin
151                src_reg = (b[0][3:2] != TILE_RED_ACC_SOURCE);
152                compute_cmd.execute.params.fn = compute_function_u'(src_reg ? b[0][3:2] : b[1][4:3]);
153                compute_cmd.execute.params.field_width = {1'b0,b[0][1:0],b[1][7:5]} + 6'd1;
154                ra = src_reg ? b[1][4:0] : 5'd0;
                                -8-  
                                ==>  
                                ==>  
155              end
156              TILE_OP_IDXR: begin
157                ra = {2'b0,b[0][3:1]};
                   ==>
158                rb = b[1][7:3];
159                flag0 = b[0][0];
160                compute_cmd.execute.params.field_width = 6'd1 << b[1][2:0];
161                compute_cmd.execute.params.arg.bit_offset = {2'b0,b[1][2:0]};
162                rd = ra;
163                dst_reg = b[0][0];
164              end
165              TILE_OP_DESC, TILE_OP_DESC_C: begin
166                commit_req.cmte.command = CMTE_CMD_DESC;
167                commit_req.cmte.amode = b[0][3:2];
168                commit_req.cmte.commit = b[0][7];
169                commit_req.cmte.wide = b[0][1];
170                commit_req.cmte.cell_width_m1 = b[1][7:3];
171                commit_req.cmte.num_cells_m1 = b[1][2:0];
172                if (b[0][0]) begin
                   -9-  
173                  commit_req.cmte.no_compare = b[2][7];
                     ==>
174                  commit_req.cmte.guarded = b[2][6];
175                  commit_req.slot = b[2][5:4];
176                end
                   MISSING_ELSE
                   ==>
177                case (tile_amode_e'(b[0][3:2]))
                   -10-

                     ==>
179                  TILE_AMODE_NEAR: begin

                       ==>

182                  end
183                  TILE_AMODE_ABS: begin
184                    commit_req.cmte.desc_address = {descriptor_tail[0],descriptor_tail[1],descriptor_tail[2]};
                       ==>
185                    descriptor_wide_byte = descriptor_tail[3];
186                  end
187                  default: begin end
                     ==>
188                endcase
189                if (b[0][1]) begin
                   -11-

                     ==>
191                  commit_req.cmte.num_cells_m1 = '0;
192                  commit_req.cmte.limb_count_m1 = descriptor_wide_byte[7:5];
193                  commit_req.cmte.last_bits_m1 = descriptor_wide_byte[4:0];
194                end
                   MISSING_ELSE
                   ==>
195              end
196              TILE_OP_SYS: case (tile_sys_subop_e'(b[0][3:0]))
                              -12-  
197                TILE_SYS_ENDL: load_cmd.kind = LOAD_END;
                   ==>
198                TILE_SYS_LOADI: begin end
                   ==>

                   ==>

                     ==>

                     ==>

                     ==>

221                end

                   ==>
223              endcase

                 ==>

245        1/1              compute_cmd.execute.fu.shift_arithmetic = (op == TILE_OP_SHIFT && flag1) ||
246                                                     (op == TILE_OP_LDX && flag0);
247        1/1              compute_cmd.execute.fu.shift_rotate = op == TILE_OP_ROT;
248        1/1              compute_cmd.execute.fu.shift_ldx = op == TILE_OP_LDX;
249        1/1              compute_cmd.execute.fu.logic_uniform = op == TILE_OP_LOGU;
250        1/1              compute_cmd.execute.fu.logic_local = op == TILE_OP_LOGL;
251        1/1              compute_cmd.execute.fu.mode = flag0;
252        1/1              compute_cmd.execute.fu.arithmetic_compare = op == TILE_OP_CMP;
253        1/1              compute_cmd.execute.fu.arithmetic_chain = flag1;
254        1/1              compute_cmd.execute.fu.arithmetic_enable = op == TILE_OP_ARITH;
255        1/1              compute_cmd.execute.fu.multiply_enable = op == TILE_OP_SYS && sub == TILE_SYS_MUL;
256        1/1              compute_cmd.execute.fu.result_sel = TILE_FU_NONE;
257        1/1              case (tile_opcode_e'(op))
258                           TILE_OP_LOADB, TILE_OP_SETM, TILE_OP_LOGU, TILE_OP_LOGL,
259        1/1                TILE_OP_ST, TILE_OP_DESC, TILE_OP_DESC_C: ; // No result-bearing functional unit.
260        1/1                TILE_OP_ARITH, TILE_OP_CMP: compute_cmd.execute.fu.result_sel = TILE_FU_ARITH;
261        1/1                TILE_OP_SHIFT, TILE_OP_ROT, TILE_OP_LDX, TILE_OP_STI: compute_cmd.execute.fu.result_sel = TILE_FU_SHIFT;
262        1/1                TILE_OP_IDXR: compute_cmd.execute.fu.result_sel = TILE_FU_INDEX;
263        1/1                TILE_OP_RED: compute_cmd.execute.fu.result_sel = TILE_FU_REDUCE;
264        2/2                TILE_OP_SYS: if (compute_cmd.execute.fu.multiply_enable) compute_cmd.execute.fu.result_sel = TILE_FU_MULTIPLY;
                        MISSING_ELSE
265        1/1                default: ;
266                         endcase
267                     
268                         // INSTRUCTION_REFERENCE.md sections 3.2-3.9: the RF stays two-read/one-write.
269                         // An RF destination is read separately after operand capture, before merge.
270        1/1              compute_cmd.capture.rs1_sel = ra;
271        1/1              compute_cmd.capture.rs2_sel = rb;
272        1/1              compute_cmd.execute.wb.rd_sel = rd;
273        1/1              compute_cmd.execute.wb.mask_sel = mm;
274        1/1              compute_cmd.capture.x_sel = X_ACC;
275        1/1              compute_cmd.execute.wb.dest_sel = dst_reg ? DEST_RF : DEST_ACC;
276        1/1              compute_cmd.execute.wb.merge_op = MERGE_MASKED;
277        1/1              compute_cmd.execute.wb.full_mask = 1'b1;
278        1/1              compute_cmd.capture.immediate = {b[1],b[2],b[3],b[4]};
279        1/1              case (tile_opcode_e'(op))
280                           TILE_OP_SETM: begin
281        1/1                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.write_enable = 1'b1;
282        1/1                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.dest_sel = DEST_MASK;
283        1/1                  compute_cmd.capture.x_sel = X_IMMEDIATE;
284        1/1                  if (!flag0) begin
285        1/1                    compute_cmd.capture.immediate = WORD_WIDTH'(mask_nibble) << WORD_IDX'(compute_cmd.execute.params.arg.bit_offset * NIBBLE_WIDTH);
286        1/1                    compute_cmd.execute.wb.merge_op = MERGE_FIELD;
287                             end
                        MISSING_ELSE
288                           end
289                           TILE_OP_LOGU, TILE_OP_LOGL: begin
290        1/1                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.write_enable = 1'b1;
291        1/1                  compute_cmd.capture.rs1_sel = rb;
292        1/1                  compute_cmd.capture.rs2_sel = rc;
293        1/1                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.full_mask = 1'b0;
294        1/1                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.lane_mask = op == TILE_OP_LOGU && flag0;
295                           end
296                           TILE_OP_ST, TILE_OP_STI: begin
297        1/1                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.write_enable = 1'b1;
298        1/1                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.full_mask = 1'b0;
299        1/1                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.result_arithmetic = op == TILE_OP_STI;
300                           end
301                           TILE_OP_LDX, TILE_OP_ROT: begin
302        1/1                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.write_enable = 1'b1;
303        1/1                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.result_arithmetic = 1'b1;
304                           end
305                           TILE_OP_ARITH, TILE_OP_CMP, TILE_OP_SHIFT: begin
306        1/1                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.write_enable = 1'b1;
307        1/1                  compute_cmd.capture.x_sel = X_RS2;
308        1/1                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.full_mask = 1'b0;
309        1/1                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.result_arithmetic = 1'b1;
310                           end
311                           TILE_OP_RED: begin
312        1/1                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.write_enable = 1'b1;
313        1/1                  compute_cmd.capture.x_sel = src_reg ? X_RS1 : X_ACC;
314        1/1                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.result_arithmetic = 1'b1;
315        1/1                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.merge_op = MERGE_FIELD;
316                           end
317                           TILE_OP_IDXR: begin
318        1/1                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.write_enable = 1'b1;
319        1/1                  compute_cmd.capture.rs1_sel = rb;
320        1/1                  compute_cmd.capture.rs2_sel = ra;
321        1/1                  compute_cmd.capture.x_sel = flag0 ? X_ACC : X_RS2;
322        1/1                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.result_arithmetic = 1'b1;
323        1/1                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.index_bounds = 1'b1;
324        1/1                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.merge_op = flag0 ? MERGE_FIELD : MERGE_ZERO_EXT;
325                           end
326        1/1                TILE_OP_SYS: case (tile_sys_subop_e'(sub))
327                             TILE_SYS_LOADI: begin
328        1/1                    compute_cmd.execute.wb.write_enable = 1'b1;
329        1/1                    compute_cmd.capture.x_sel = X_IMMEDIATE;
330                             end
331                             TILE_SYS_LD: begin
332        1/1                    compute_cmd.execute.wb.write_enable = 1'b1;
333        1/1                    compute_cmd.capture.x_sel = X_RS1;
334        1/1                    compute_cmd.capture.rs2_sel = TILE_GUARD_REG;
335        1/1                    compute_cmd.execute.wb.publish = flag0;
336                             end
337                             TILE_SYS_MOV: begin
338        1/1                    compute_cmd.execute.wb.write_enable = 1'b1;
339        1/1                    compute_cmd.capture.x_sel = X_RS1;
340        1/1                    compute_cmd.execute.wb.full_mask = 1'b0;
341                             end
342                             TILE_SYS_MUL: begin
343        1/1                    compute_cmd.execute.wb.write_enable = 1'b1;
344        1/1                    compute_cmd.capture.x_sel = X_RS2;
345        1/1                    compute_cmd.execute.wb.result_arithmetic = 1'b1;
346                             end
347        1/1                  default: ;
348                           endcase
349        1/1                default: ;
350                         endcase
351        1/1              cmd_o.dispatch = dispatch;
352        2/2              if (dispatch.target.load) cmd_o.payload.load.cmd = load_cmd;
353        2/2              else if (dispatch.target.commit) cmd_o.payload.commit.cmd = commit_req;
354        2/2              else if (dispatch.target.index_push) cmd_o.payload.index_push.cmd = index_push_cmd;
355        1/1              else cmd_o.payload.compute = compute_cmd;

                   -9-

                     ==>

                   MISSING_ELSE
                   ==>

                   -10-

                     ==>

                       ==>

182                  end
183                  TILE_AMODE_ABS: begin
184                    commit_req.cmte.desc_address = {descriptor_tail[0],descriptor_tail[1],descriptor_tail[2]};
                       ==>

186                  end
187                  default: begin end
                     ==>
188                endcase
189                if (b[0][1]) begin
                   -11-

                     ==>
191                  commit_req.cmte.num_cells_m1 = '0;

194                end
                   MISSING_ELSE
                   ==>
195              end
196              TILE_OP_SYS: case (tile_sys_subop_e'(b[0][3:0]))
                              -12-  
197                TILE_SYS_ENDL: load_cmd.kind = LOAD_END;
                   ==>
198                TILE_SYS_LOADI: begin end
                   ==>
199                TILE_SYS_ENDC: commit_req.cmte.command = CMTE_CMD_ENDC;
                   ==>
200                TILE_SYS_IDXPUSH_C: index_push_cmd.base_address = {b[1],b[2],b[3]};
                   ==>
201                TILE_SYS_IDXPUSH_L: begin
202                  load_cmd.kind = LOAD_PUSH_INDEX_ADDRESS;
                     ==>
203                  load_cmd.address = {b[1],b[2],b[3]};
204                end
205                TILE_SYS_SETC: begin
206                  commit_req.cmte.command = CMTE_CMD_SETC;
                     ==>
207                  commit_req.cmte.cursor_pos = b[1][7:3];
208                end
209                TILE_SYS_LD: begin
210                  ra = b[1][7:3];
                     ==>
211                  flag0 = b[1][2]; // LD.b supplies the commit value/guards.
212                end
213                TILE_SYS_MOV: begin
214                  dst_reg = 1;
                     ==>
215                  rd = b[1][7:3];
216                  ra = b[2][7:3];
217                end
218                TILE_SYS_MUL: begin
219                  dst_reg = b[1][1];
                     ==>
220                  flag0 = b[1][2];
221                end
222                default: ;
                   ==>
223              endcase
224              default: ;
                 ==>

279            case (tile_opcode_e'(op))
               -1-  
280              TILE_OP_SETM: begin
281                compute_cmd.execute.wb.write_enable = 1'b1;
282                compute_cmd.execute.wb.dest_sel = DEST_MASK;
283                compute_cmd.capture.x_sel = X_IMMEDIATE;
284                if (!flag0) begin
                   -2-  
285                  compute_cmd.capture.immediate = WORD_WIDTH'(mask_nibble) << WORD_IDX'(compute_cmd.execute.params.arg.bit_offset * NIBBLE_WIDTH);
                     ==>
286                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.merge_op = MERGE_FIELD;
287                end
                   MISSING_ELSE
                   ==>
288              end
289              TILE_OP_LOGU, TILE_OP_LOGL: begin
290                compute_cmd.execute.wb.write_enable = 1'b1;
                   ==>
291                compute_cmd.capture.rs1_sel = rb;
292                compute_cmd.capture.rs2_sel = rc;
293                compute_cmd.execute.wb.full_mask = 1'b0;
294                compute_cmd.execute.wb.lane_mask = op == TILE_OP_LOGU && flag0;
295              end
296              TILE_OP_ST, TILE_OP_STI: begin
297                compute_cmd.execute.wb.write_enable = 1'b1;
                   ==>
298                compute_cmd.execute.wb.full_mask = 1'b0;
299                compute_cmd.execute.wb.result_arithmetic = op == TILE_OP_STI;
300              end
301              TILE_OP_LDX, TILE_OP_ROT: begin
302                compute_cmd.execute.wb.write_enable = 1'b1;
                   ==>
303                compute_cmd.execute.wb.result_arithmetic = 1'b1;
304              end
305              TILE_OP_ARITH, TILE_OP_CMP, TILE_OP_SHIFT: begin
306                compute_cmd.execute.wb.write_enable = 1'b1;
                   ==>
307                compute_cmd.capture.x_sel = X_RS2;
308                compute_cmd.execute.wb.full_mask = 1'b0;
309                compute_cmd.execute.wb.result_arithmetic = 1'b1;
310              end
311              TILE_OP_RED: begin
312                compute_cmd.execute.wb.write_enable = 1'b1;
313                compute_cmd.capture.x_sel = src_reg ? X_RS1 : X_ACC;
                                                       -3-  
                                                       ==>  
                                                       ==>  
314                compute_cmd.execute.wb.result_arithmetic = 1'b1;
315                compute_cmd.execute.wb.merge_op = MERGE_FIELD;
316              end
317              TILE_OP_IDXR: begin
318                compute_cmd.execute.wb.write_enable = 1'b1;
319                compute_cmd.capture.rs1_sel = rb;
320                compute_cmd.capture.rs2_sel = ra;
321                compute_cmd.capture.x_sel = flag0 ? X_ACC : X_RS2;
                                                     -4-  
                                                     ==>  
                                                     ==>  
322                compute_cmd.execute.wb.result_arithmetic = 1'b1;
323                compute_cmd.execute.wb.index_bounds = 1'b1;
324                compute_cmd.execute.wb.merge_op = flag0 ? MERGE_FIELD : MERGE_ZERO_EXT;
                                                           -5-  
                                                           ==>  
                                                           ==>  
325              end
326              TILE_OP_SYS: case (tile_sys_subop_e'(sub))
                              -6-  
327                TILE_SYS_LOADI: begin
328                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.write_enable = 1'b1;
                     ==>
329                  compute_cmd.capture.x_sel = X_IMMEDIATE;
330                end
331                TILE_SYS_LD: begin
332                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.write_enable = 1'b1;
                     ==>
333                  compute_cmd.capture.x_sel = X_RS1;
334                  compute_cmd.capture.rs2_sel = TILE_GUARD_REG;
335                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.publish = flag0;
336                end
337                TILE_SYS_MOV: begin
338                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.write_enable = 1'b1;
                     ==>
339                  compute_cmd.capture.x_sel = X_RS1;
340                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.full_mask = 1'b0;
341                end
342                TILE_SYS_MUL: begin
343                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.write_enable = 1'b1;
                     ==>
344                  compute_cmd.capture.x_sel = X_RS2;
345                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.result_arithmetic = 1'b1;
346                end
347                default: ;
                   ==>
348              endcase
349              default: ;
                 ==>
